[Review](ErrorDialogStyleAlignment/R1/ErrorDialog_Alignment_R1_Review.html) · [Implementation/API/limits](ErrorDialogStyleAlignment/R1/ImplementationReport.md) · [Provenance](ErrorDialogStyleAlignment/R1/AssetProvenance.json).

`ScreenRouter.ShowErrorDialog(Action retryFailedOperation=null)` accepts a real failed-operation callback. It runs once after modal close; intervening navigation cancels it. Without callback, Retry is disabled with an honest fallback message. Support is disabled/unavailable until configured. Close/systemBack preserve the underlying page instance. Generic `Show(EmptyErrorState)` opens this modal; no fake Home-as-Retry behavior remains. Existing modal/screen transitions reject duplicate requests. Gameplay is explicitly excluded from this API for now, preserving scope; service/async integration remains future work.
